    LIMIT 1), (
    SELECT `t2`.`present`
    FROM (
        SELECT `y2`.`report_id`, `y2`.`absent`, `y2`.`attendanceRate`, `y2`.`average_absent`, `y2`.`average_late`, `y2`.`average_leave`, `y2`.`average_present`, `y2`.`average_undertime`, `y2`.`late`, `y2`.`leave_count`, `y2`.`present`, `y2`.`underTime`, `y2`.`year`, 1 AS `Key`
        FROM `year_attendance_report` AS `y2`
        WHERE `y2`.`year` = @__year_0
    ) AS `t2`
    WHERE `t`.`Key` = `t2`.`Key`
    LIMIT 1), (
    SELECT `t3`.`underTime`
    FROM (
        SELECT `y3`.`report_id`, `y3`.`absent`, `y3`.`attendanceRate`, `y3`.`average_absent`, `y3`.`average_late`, `y3`.`average_leave`, `y3`.`average_present`, `y3`.`average_undertime`, `y3`.`late`, `y3`.`leave_count`, `y3`.`present`, `y3`.`underTime`, `y3`.`year`, 1 AS `Key`
        FROM `year_attendance_report` AS `y3`
        WHERE `y3`.`year` = @__year_0
    ) AS `t3`
    WHERE `t`.`Key` = `t3`.`Key`
    LIMIT 1), (
    SELECT `t4`.`year`
    FROM (
        SELECT `y4`.`report_id`, `y4`.`absent`, `y4`.`attendanceRate`, `y4`.`average_absent`, `y4`.`average_late`, `y4`.`average_leave`, `y4`.`average_present`, `y4`.`average_undertime`, `y4`.`late`, `y4`.`leave_count`, `y4`.`present`, `y4`.`underTime`, `y4`.`year`, 1 AS `Key`
        FROM `year_attendance_report` AS `y4`
        WHERE `y4`.`year` = @__year_0
    ) AS `t4`
    WHERE `t`.`Key` = `t4`.`Key`
    LIMIT 1)
FROM (
    SELECT 1 AS `Key`
    FROM `year_attendance_report` AS `y`
    WHERE `y`.`year` = @__year_0
) AS `t`
GROUP BY `t`.`Key`
LIMIT 1
